## Summary

These contracts accept signatures only from a key fixed in their code (operator, issuer, quote or claim signer). 782 code families, 209,205 addresses, $3.9M held, 276,478 recoveries in the study's two-week window. Rows by rating: HIGH 716, MEDIUM 39, LOW 27. 97 rows were checked on a real transaction; the others were read from source, judged from bytecode or placed by measured rules (each row's note says which).

## Mechanism

PQ-17. Once that key's account has real code, every signed function stops. Users' funds behind it are stuck unless another path releases them; where only the operator's own money is at stake the operator withdraws and redeploys.

## Evidence

- Each row's note says how it was decided: read from verified source, judged from bytecode and function names, placed by a rule measured against hand readings, or executed: a real transaction replayed on the fork's reference implementation with only the precompile changed, state at block 26,438,999.

## Impact and exposure

Largest rows by value held at block 26,438,999:
- `0x66b71dc6f3c3ab6ffab508d9b5266f68bb39e67c` (AmbireAccount7702): $3.5M, LOW, 172,117 addresses
- `0x6e653024fe23e4a7f2a1c8e14c388f71dd44668f` (unverified): $134k, HIGH, 204 addresses
- `0x44b317be48a42bbe6445dc62c4681a95a278b417` (unverified): $74k, HIGH, 91 addresses
- `0x066ccd23e6f16072b6b658a65d1a0cdb59b50115` (unverified): $40k, HIGH, 31 addresses
- `0xaaaaaaceeed2b24ef8083f8703c6d6f3a36e1137` (unverified): $20k, LOW
- `0xf9f4ba80606cd2ac0ceb16d47fdacb9843ce6ff5` (unverified): $13k, HIGH, 47 addresses
- `0xbe95329b0882b2a6e95a0092483a131d63c9de18` (unverified): $13k, HIGH, 20 addresses
- `0x16f78145ad0b9af58747e9a97ebd99175378bd3d` (GroupDAO): $8k, HIGH, 2 addresses
- `0xe7b0ce0526fbe3969035a145c9e9691d4d9d216c` (ClipperVerifiedCaravelExchange): $7k, HIGH
- `0x53a449540d792dc8f848d8a050e003ef63e348cc` (unverified): $7k, HIGH

Every row is in the header; `generated/triage_contracts.csv` lists them for search.

## Remediation

The operator keeps the signing key without code, or moves users to a new deployment.
